<<<INPUT: contract>>>
# CONTRACT — Inventory / المخزون (INV) — outbound promise
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Module : INV   Version : v1   Profile : commerce   Stage : P1.5
Inputs : srs-inv.md (P1 pass 1, round 1), registry-srs (P1 — INV v1)
Counts : CON 21 (ENTITY 5 · OPERATION 16) · ADR 0 (next free ADR-INV-014)
Status : DRAFT — P1.5 pass 1, round 1 · prepared by claude:opus
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## 0 — Reading this contract

- This is what other modules may depend on before INV is built: the SHARED entities they
  reference and the operations and events they call or receive. It promises; it never
  implements. The INV backend plan (P3.1) chooses paths and verbs and states
  `Honours: CON-INV-…` on the block that implements each item.
- An item with a `Signature` line is an operation (or a published event); an item without one
  is an entity promise.
- **Identifiers.** Every INV entity identifier is a `BIGINT` identity key assigned by the
  database (stack.db.pk_generation = identity). A consumer's HARD-FK column to an INV entity is
  `BIGINT` — copied verbatim, no other identifier type exists in v1.
- **Not promised (private):** Stock move (ENT-INV-004 — published only as the event of
  CON-INV-020), Reservation (ENT-INV-005 — reached only through CON-INV-011 … 013, 015),
  Transfer and its lines (ENT-INV-006, 007), Stock count and its lines (ENT-INV-008, 009),
  Stock adjustment and its lines (ENT-INV-010, 011), Item stock settings (ENT-INV-014 — read
  only through CON-INV-018). No table, column list beyond key columns, or endpoint path is
  promised.
- **Conventions binding every operation (from the SRS):**
  - Calls are in-process (`crossmodule` APIs); the calling module authorises its own caller;
    tenancy applies to every call (SRS Access summary).
  - Quantities are in the Item's base unit (CON-CAT-001 `baseUomCode`); the caller converts
    other units first (SRS D-05). Quantity and measure: 3 decimals; unit and average cost:
    4 decimals; value: 2 decimals in the tenant base currency (SRS D-06).
  - A warehouse input is optional while the tenant has exactly one active warehouse (the
    default is applied) and required when two or more are active (RULE-INV-008, REQ-INV-013,
    REQ-INV-017).
  - When `variants` is off the caller passes the Item's default Variant (CON-CAT-002); INV
    always keys on a Variant (SRS D-27).
  - Every receipt, issue and reservation request carries a caller-supplied idempotency key
    (text, ≤ 64); a key repeated with the same request returns the first result and records
    nothing new (RULE-INV-024, REQ-INV-058) — this is not an error. A key repeated with a different
    request is refused, so a caller never mistakes a new request for one already recorded
    (RULE-INV-050).
  - Source documents are passed as type (INV_SOURCE_DOC_TYPE — open lookup; calling modules add
    their document types as data), identifier (BIGINT), number (text ≤ 30) and optional line
    identifier (BIGINT). INV keeps them as SOFT references with no FK (ENT-INV-004.sourceDocRef).
  - Errors are stated by meaning; each carries its RULE's Arabic and English message (SRS A5).
  - Capability flags (`lotExpiry`, `dualQuantity`, `bundles`) are read by INV through the
    FeatureFlags API; behaviour marked "flag on" applies only while the flag is on (REQ-INV-132).

## 1 — ENTITY items

### CON-INV-001 — المستودع / Warehouse (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-INV-001 — key columns: `code` (business key, series INV_WAREHOUSE, read-only, text 20), `warehousePk` (identifier a consumer stores) · identifier type: BIGINT
Consumers : PUR, SLS, SFR, INS (HARD-FK `BIGINT` → warehousePk)
Promise   : a deactivated Warehouse stays readable by its identifier and is excluded from every
            new selection; consumers are notified through CON-INV-021 and their records are never
            changed (notify, never cascade — RULE-INV-005). Exactly one active default Warehouse
            exists per tenant from provisioning on (REQ-INV-001, RULE-INV-006).
Traces    : ENT-INV-001, REQ-INV-001, REQ-INV-005, REQ-INV-009, REQ-INV-019

### CON-INV-002 — الفرع / Branch (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-INV-002 — key columns: `code` (business key, series INV_BRANCH, read-only, text 20), `branchPk` (identifier a consumer stores) · identifier type: BIGINT
Consumers : PUR, SLS, STL, TAX, INS (HARD-FK `BIGINT` → branchPk)
Promise   : a deactivated Branch stays readable by its identifier and is excluded from every new
            selection; consumers are notified through CON-INV-021 and their records are never
            changed (RULE-INV-014). Exactly one active default Branch exists per tenant from
            provisioning on (REQ-INV-020, RULE-INV-015). Every Warehouse belongs to a Branch
            (ENT-INV-001.branchFk).
Traces    : ENT-INV-002, REQ-INV-020, REQ-INV-024, REQ-INV-028, REQ-INV-033

### CON-INV-003 — التشغيلة / Lot (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-INV-012 — key columns: `variantFk` + `code` (business key — the lot number written on the goods, unique per Variant, text 40), `lotPk` (identifier a consumer stores) · identifier type: BIGINT
Consumers : PUR (receipt), SLS (FEFO issue) — flag `lotExpiry` on
Promise   : one lot number of a Variant has one expiry date (RULE-INV-036); a Lot is created by
            INV on the first receipt or increase that names it (REQ-INV-106) — consumers never
            create a Lot directly. A Lot whose expiry date is before today (tenant time zone) is
            never sold (RULE-INV-038).
Traces    : ENT-INV-012, REQ-INV-106, REQ-INV-109, REQ-INV-113

### CON-INV-004 — رصيد المخزون / Stock balance (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-INV-003 — key columns: `variantFk` + `warehouseFk` + `lotFk` (business key — one record per Variant × warehouse × lot, lot empty for stock without a lot), `stockBalancePk` (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Consumers : SLS, SFR, INS (read through CON-INV-010; no consumer stores stockBalancePk)
Promise   : one stock for every channel (REQ-INV-035); maintained only by stock moves, never
            written by another module. Reserved and available quantities are derived at read time
            (REQ-INV-038).
Traces    : ENT-INV-003, REQ-INV-035, REQ-INV-038, REQ-INV-040

### CON-INV-005 — تقييم المخزون / Stock valuation (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-INV-013 — key columns: `variantFk` (business key — one record per Variant across the tenant), `stockValuationPk` (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Consumers : SLS (margin), INS (read through CON-INV-016); ACC receives costs through CON-INV-020
Promise   : perpetual weighted-average cost per Variant across the tenant (REQ-INV-059 …
            REQ-INV-062); maintained only by stock moves.
Traces    : ENT-INV-013, REQ-INV-059, REQ-INV-063

## 2 — OPERATION items

### CON-INV-006 — قراءة مستودع / Read a warehouse (ar / en)
Signature : readWarehouse(warehousePk: BIGINT) → Warehouse {warehousePk, code, nameAr, nameEn, branchPk, isDefaultFl, isActiveFl} · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-INV-001
Notes     : returns inactive Warehouses too (RULE-INV-005).
Traces    : REQ-INV-009, REQ-INV-005

### CON-INV-007 — المستودعات النشطة / List the active warehouses (ar / en)
Signature : listActiveWarehouses() → {activeCount, warehouses: [{warehousePk, code, nameAr, nameEn, branchPk, isDefaultFl}]} · errors: none
Entity    : ENT-INV-001
Notes     : consumers hide their own warehouse selector while activeCount is 1 (POL-INV-004).
Traces    : REQ-INV-019

### CON-INV-008 — قراءة فرع / Read a branch (ar / en)
Signature : readBranch(branchPk: BIGINT) → Branch {branchPk, code, nameAr, nameEn, isDefaultFl, isActiveFl} · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-INV-002
Notes     : returns inactive Branches too (RULE-INV-014).
Traces    : REQ-INV-028, REQ-INV-024

### CON-INV-009 — الفروع النشطة / List the active branches (ar / en)
Signature : listActiveBranches() → {activeCount, branches: [{branchPk, code, nameAr, nameEn, isDefaultFl}]} · errors: none
Entity    : ENT-INV-002
Notes     : consumers hide their own branch selector while activeCount is 1 (POL-INV-016).
Traces    : REQ-INV-033

### CON-INV-010 — توفّر متغيّر الصنف / Read the availability of a Variant (ar / en)
Signature : readAvailability(variantPk: BIGINT, warehousePk?: BIGINT) → {perWarehouse: [{warehousePk, onHandQty, reservedQty, availableQty, onHandMeasureQty?, measureUomCode?}], total: {onHandQty, reservedQty, availableQty, onHandMeasureQty?}} · errors: Variant not found
Entity    : ENT-INV-003
Notes     : per active warehouse and in total. Available = on-hand outside expired lots minus
            ACTIVE reservations (RULE-INV-038). A Variant that never moved answers zeros, not an
            error (REQ-INV-041). Flag `bundles` on: a bundle Variant answers the derived
            availability of its components (REQ-INV-042). Flag `dualQuantity` on: measure and its
            unit for dual-quantity Items (REQ-INV-121).
Traces    : REQ-INV-040, REQ-INV-041, REQ-INV-042, REQ-INV-121

### CON-INV-011 — حجز مخزون / Reserve stock (ar / en)
Signature : reserve(variantPk: BIGINT, warehousePk?: BIGINT, quantity, sourceDoc {typeCode, ref, no, lineRef?}, idempotencyKey) → {reservations: [{reservationPk: BIGINT, variantPk, warehousePk, statusCode, originalQty, reservedQty, shortfallQty, bundleVariantPk?}]} · errors: warehouse required, warehouse inactive, Variant inactive, invalid quantity, not enough available (negative stock disallowed), bundle without active components, idempotency key already used for a different request
Entity    : ENT-INV-003
Notes     : while negative stock is allowed (tenant default) a request beyond availability is
            accepted with its shortfall recorded; the caller decides what to do with it
            (REQ-INV-045, SRS D-11). Flag `bundles` on: a bundle is reserved as one Reservation per
            component (REQ-INV-049). The caller keeps reservationPk as a SOFT reference (no FK) to
            release or consume it; the Reservation itself is private. Reservations do not expire
            automatically in v1 — the owning module releases them (SRS D-24).
            RULE-INV-007, 008, 017, 018, 020, 022, 024, 050.
Traces    : REQ-INV-043, REQ-INV-044, REQ-INV-045, REQ-INV-049

### CON-INV-012 — تحرير حجز / Release a reservation (ar / en)
Signature : releaseReservation(reservationPk: BIGINT) → {reservationPk, statusCode RELEASED, reservedQty 0, closedAt} · errors: not found, reservation closed
Entity    : ENT-INV-003
Notes     : only an ACTIVE reservation is released (RULE-INV-021); availability rises at once.
Traces    : REQ-INV-046, REQ-INV-048

### CON-INV-013 — حجوزات مستند / Read the reservations of a source document (ar / en)
Signature : reservationsOfDocument(sourceDocTypeCode, sourceDocRef: BIGINT) → [{reservationPk, variantPk, warehousePk, statusCode, reservedQty, shortfallQty, bundleVariantPk?}] · errors: none (empty list when none)
Entity    : ENT-INV-003
Traces    : REQ-INV-050

### CON-INV-014 — استلام مخزون / Receive stock (ar / en)
Signature : receiveStock(warehousePk?: BIGINT, moveTypeCode RECEIPT | RETURN_IN, lines: [{variantPk, quantity, unitCost?, lotNo?, lotExpiryDate?, measureQty?, sourceLineRef?}], sourceDoc {typeCode, ref, no}, idempotencyKey) → {moves: [{stockMovePk: BIGINT, variantPk, warehousePk, lotPk?, quantity, measureQty?, unitCost, moveValue}]} · errors: warehouse required, warehouse inactive, Variant inactive or of a BUNDLE Item, invalid quantity, no known cost, lot number and expiry required, lot recorded with a different expiry, measure required, idempotency key already used for a different request
Entity    : ENT-INV-003
Notes     : one move per line at the line's unit cost; a line without a cost takes the current
            average and is refused when the Variant was never valued (RULE-INV-025). Each receipt
            recalculates the weighted-average cost (REQ-INV-059). All lines are recorded or none.
            Flag `lotExpiry` on: lot-tracked Items require lot number and expiry; the Lot is reused
            or created (REQ-INV-105, REQ-INV-106). Flag `dualQuantity` on: dual-quantity Items
            require the measure (REQ-INV-120). Whether an Item needs a lot or a measure is read
            beforehand through CON-INV-018. A bundle Variant is never received or returned in: the
            line is refused as "of a BUNDLE Item" (RULE-INV-018). RULE-INV-007, 008, 017, 018, 024,
            025, 035, 036, 040, 050.
Traces    : REQ-INV-051, REQ-INV-064, REQ-INV-105, REQ-INV-106, REQ-INV-107, REQ-INV-120

### CON-INV-015 — صرف مخزون / Issue stock (ar / en)
Signature : issueStock(warehousePk?: BIGINT, moveTypeCode ISSUE | RETURN_OUT, saleFl, offlineFl, lines: [{variantPk, quantity, lotPk? (non-sale only), measureQty?, reservationPk?, sourceLineRef?}], sourceDoc {typeCode, ref, no}, idempotencyKey) → {moves: [{stockMovePk: BIGINT, variantPk, warehousePk, lotPk?, quantity, measureQty?, unitCost, moveValue, bundleVariantPk?}]} · errors: warehouse required, warehouse inactive, Variant inactive, invalid quantity, not enough stock (online, negative stock disallowed), reservation closed, reservation not of this line (Variant, warehouse or source document differs), bundle without active components, lot expired, measure required, idempotency key already used for a different request
Entity    : ENT-INV-003
Notes     : moves are valued at the Variant's current average cost (REQ-INV-061). An issue that
            references a reservation consumes it (REQ-INV-047). A synchronised offline POS sale
            (offlineFl true) is always recorded, whatever the negative-stock setting
            (REQ-INV-069). Flag `bundles` on: a bundle is issued as one move per component and
            none for the bundle itself (REQ-INV-053). Flag `lotExpiry` on: a sale issue draws stock
            without a lot first, then unexpired lots by nearest expiry, and posts any uncovered
            quantity without a lot (REQ-INV-110 … 113); a non-sale issue or return to supplier
            naming an expired lot is refused (REQ-INV-114). One answer may therefore carry several
            moves per line. All lines are recorded or none.
            A referenced reservation must be of the line's Variant and warehouse and of the request's source
            document (RULE-INV-049). A repeated idempotency key submitted concurrently waits for the first
            submission and returns its answer.
            While negative stock is disallowed, a line that names a lot is also refused when that lot's
            on-hand in the warehouse does not cover it (RULE-INV-019).
            RULE-INV-007, 008, 017, 018, 019, 021, 022, 024, 038, 039, 040, 049, 050.
Traces    : REQ-INV-052, REQ-INV-053, REQ-INV-067, REQ-INV-069, REQ-INV-110, REQ-INV-111, REQ-INV-114

### CON-INV-016 — قراءة التقييم / Read the valuation of a Variant (ar / en)
Signature : readValuation(variantPk: BIGINT) → {variantPk, totalOnHandQty, averageUnitCost, stockValue} · errors: Variant not found
Entity    : ENT-INV-013
Notes     : a Variant never valued answers zero quantity and value with no average (consistent
            with REQ-INV-041; the caller treats a missing average as "no known cost").
Traces    : REQ-INV-063

### CON-INV-017 — قراءة تشغيلة / Read a lot (ar / en)
Signature : readLot(lotPk: BIGINT) → Lot {lotPk, variantPk, code, nameAr, nameEn, expiryDate, expiredFl, isActiveFl} · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-INV-012
Traces    : REQ-INV-109

### CON-INV-018 — إعدادات مخزون الصنف / Read the stock settings of an Item (ar / en)
Signature : readItemStockSettings(itemPk: BIGINT) → {lotTrackedFl, dualQuantityFl, measureUomCode?} · errors: none (false, false, empty when the Item has no record)
Entity    : ENT-INV-014 (read-only answer; the entity itself stays private)
Traces    : REQ-INV-129

### CON-INV-019 — فحص إيقاف مفتاح القدرة / Capability turn-off check (ar / en)
Signature : canTurnOff(flag lotExpiry | dualQuantity) → {allowedFl, dependentCount, dependents: [{itemPk, code, nameAr, nameEn}] (at most 20), messageAr?, messageEn?} · errors: unknown flag
Entity    : ENT-INV-014 (read-only answer)
Notes     : called by the capability-flag owner (P-02); not allowed while any Item depends on the
            flag, with a message naming the next step (RULE-INV-046).
Traces    : REQ-INV-130, REQ-INV-131

### CON-INV-020 — حدث حركة المخزون / Stock-move event (ar / en)
Signature : event StockMoveRecorded {stockMovePk: BIGINT, docDate, moveTypeCode, variantPk, warehousePk, lotPk?, quantity, measureQty?, unitCost, moveValue, currencyCode, sourceDocTypeCode, sourceDocRef, sourceDocNo, sourceLineRef?, bundleVariantPk?, offlineFl} → delivered once per committed move, after commit · errors: none (never delivered when the transaction rolls back)
Entity    : ENT-INV-013 (the cost it carries); the move itself stays private
Notes     : consumers ACC (posting), SWR, INS; stockMovePk lets a consumer process each event
            once. Moves are never changed or deleted (RULE-INV-023), so no correction event exists —
            a correction arrives as a new ADJUSTMENT_IN / ADJUSTMENT_OUT move. `currencyCode` (ISO
            4217, the tenant base currency of unitCost and moveValue) is ADDITIVE in v1 (ADR-INV-015).
Traces    : REQ-INV-057, REQ-INV-055

### CON-INV-021 — حدث تغيّر بيانات المخزون الأساسية / Inventory master-change event (ar / en)
Signature : event InventoryMasterChanged {entity ENT-INV-001 | ENT-INV-002, identifier: BIGINT, changeKindCode (INV_CHANGE_KIND: CREATED, UPDATED, DEACTIVATED, REACTIVATED, DEFAULT_CHANGED)} → delivered once per committed Warehouse or Branch change, after commit · errors: none (never delivered on rollback)
Entity    : ENT-INV-001, ENT-INV-002
Notes     : consumers PUR, SLS, SFR, STL, TAX, INS refresh selectors and active counts; the event
            notifies and never cascades (ARCH-5).
Traces    : REQ-INV-034

## 2.5 — Given usage (repeated from the SRS)

```yaml name=given-usage
rows:
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.cu, via: com.erp.cu.crossmodule.SettingsApi, purpose: "read the lotExpiry, dualQuantity and bundles capability flags behind the FeatureFlags API (REQ-INV-132), read and write the INV_ALLOW_NEGATIVE_STOCK and INV_NEAR_EXPIRY_DAYS settings (REQ-INV-065, REQ-INV-118), and read the INV_MEASURE_UOM_KEY setting that names the consumed unit-of-measure lookup key (REQ-INV-125)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sequence, via: com.erp.sequence.crossmodule.NumberSeriesApi, purpose: "issue the codes INV_WAREHOUSE and INV_BRANCH and the document numbers INV_TRANSFER, INV_COUNT and INV_ADJUSTMENT (RULE-INV-001, RULE-INV-010, RULE-INV-030)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.events, via: com.erp.events.OutboxPublisher, purpose: "record the stock-move event and the inventory master-change event in the change transaction and deliver them after commit (REQ-INV-057, REQ-INV-034)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.audit, via: com.erp.audit.crossmodule.AuditApi, purpose: "record the change history of stock documents, warehouses, branches, lots and Item stock settings (REQ-INV-077)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.mdl, via: com.erp.mdl.crossmodule.MdlLookupApi, purpose: "load the values of the INV_* lookup keys and the CAT unit-of-measure key at runtime, and serve INV's six lookup keys to INV's screens through the common helper OwnedLookups.read over this interface (A6)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.tenant, via: com.erp.tenant.TenantProvisioningContributor, purpose: "seed the default branch and the default warehouse of each new tenant (REQ-INV-020, REQ-INV-001)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.tenant, via: com.erp.tenant.TenantRunner, purpose: "run the daily near-expiry check once per tenant (REQ-INV-117)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sec, via: com.erp.sec.permission.PermissionContributor, purpose: "register the INV page codes so the security module derives their VIEW/CREATE/UPDATE/DELETE permissions, and the grants of the Owner, Seller/Cashier and Warehouse role presets (REQ-INV-136, REQ-INV-137)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sec, via: com.erp.sec.crossmodule.SecUserDirectoryApi, purpose: "find the users holding the Warehouse role as recipients of the negative-stock and near-expiry notifications (REQ-INV-068, REQ-INV-117)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.notif, via: com.erp.notif.crossmodule.NotificationDispatchApi, purpose: "send the negative-stock and near-expiry notifications (REQ-INV-068, REQ-INV-117)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.report, via: com.erp.report.ReportProvider, purpose: "register the report INV_OPENING_STOCK_SHEET (REQ-INV-073)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.report, via: com.erp.report.crossmodule.PrintApi, purpose: "produce the xlsx opening stock sheet (REQ-INV-073)"}
```

The contract re-creates no given's entity: settings stay in `cu`, lookup values in `mdl`,
numbers in `sequence`, users and roles in `sec`, the tenant in `tenant`.

## 3 — Stability

- v1 is the first contract of INV; every item above is ADDITIVE. The gate-analysis revisions
  added, still ADDITIVE: the error meaning "idempotency key already used for a different
  request" on CON-INV-011, 014 and 015, the field `currencyCode` of CON-INV-020, and the stated
  refusal of a bundle receipt (CON-INV-014) and of a lot decrease beyond the lot (CON-INV-015).
- Items may be added freely. Changing or removing an item a consumer depends on — an identifier
  type, a key column, a signature input or output, an error meaning, an event field — happens
  only in a BREAKING version with an ADR; `gov.py graph` then raises a resolution event on every
  consumer edge that cites it.
- Lookup values of the open keys INV_SOURCE_DOC_TYPE and INV_ADJUSTMENT_REASON grow as data and
  are not a contract change.

## 4 — Decisions applied (no ADR raised)

Every choice here is settled by the SRS and is non-breaking; no ADR was raised and the ADR stream
continues at ADR-INV-014. No question was raised.

| # | Decision | Source |
|---|---|---|
| C-1 | ENTITY items are the SRS SHARED entities (ENT-INV-001, 002, 003, 012, 013). No other module's A8 `module-dependencies` block consumes INV yet; the consumers named are those of the SRS cross-module column and the registry. | SRS A3, A8; engine §1 |
| C-2 | OPERATION items are exactly the SRS A8 "Operations INV offers to other modules" table, one item per row, including its two published events. | SRS A8 |
| C-3 | Reservation and Stock move stay private: consumers hold `reservationPk` and `stockMovePk` only as SOFT references (no FK), returned by CON-INV-011 / 014 / 015 and carried by CON-INV-020. | SRS A3 ownership; D-23 |
| C-4 | Item stock settings stay private; its content is promised only as the read answers of CON-INV-018 and CON-INV-019. | SRS ENT-INV-014 ownership |
| C-5 | A Variant never valued answers zeros in CON-INV-016 instead of "not found", consistent with REQ-INV-041. | SRS D-10 |

<<<contract-cat.md>>>
# CONTRACT — Catalog / الكتالوج (CAT) — outbound promise
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Module : CAT   Version : v2   Profile : commerce   Stage : P1.5
Inputs : srs (P1, CAT v2 — REQ 148 · AC 174 · ENT 12 · RULE 53), registry-srs (re-stamped from the current registries at the eleventh gate-analysis revise; CON-CAT-002 wording amended by ADR-CAT-072; §4 given-usage repeated from SRS A9 as amended by ADR-CAT-076; CON-CAT-018 entity list extended by ADR-CAT-077; CON-CAT-006 and CON-CAT-007 state the unit of a Bundle component quantity — ADR-CAT-080)
Counts : CON 18 (entity items 6 · operation items 11 · event item 1)
Status : DRAFT — P1.5 pass 1 · no question raised · no ADR raised
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## 0 — Scope of this promise

This contract states what other modules may depend on in CAT before CAT is built. It is a list of promises. It contains no implementation, no table, no path and no verb. The backend stage (P3.1) chooses those and marks each implementing block with `Honours: CON-CAT-…`.

| Item | Decided by |
|---|---|
| Consumers named in the SRS | PRC, INV, PUR, SLS, SFR, INS and TAX (A3 "Cross-module" column). Also the capability-flag owner (P-02, ADR-CAT-041) and the global search host (ADR-CAT-043) |
| Entities promised | Every SHARED (owner) ENT of the SRS: ENT-CAT-001, 002, 006, 007, 008, 009 |
| Entities not promised | The PRIVATE ENTs ENT-CAT-003, 004, 005, 010, 011, 012. Consumers see their data only through the operations below, as the SRS A3 states ("read through Variant reads", "SFR reads images through Item and Variant reads", "read through Variant and Item unit reads by SLS and TAX") |
| Operations promised | The cross-module reads listed in SRS SCR-REQ-CAT-001 B5 (read Item / Variant by identifier, storefront catalogue read, Items of a Category, barcode lookup). Also the reads that the SRS A3 gives to consumers (Category for SFR/INS, Item unit for INV/PUR/SLS, Variant images for SFR), the catalogue search (ADR-CAT-043) and the turn-off check (ADR-CAT-041) |
| Event promised | The catalogue-change event of REQ-CAT-008 / RULE-CAT-049. Downstream modules react to it ("Downstream modules react to catalogue changes without CAT reading them"). The SRS states this, so it is not an open point |

**Promises that apply to every item:**
- **Identifiers.** Every identifier is a BIGINT identity key that the database assigns (stack.db.pk_generation = identity). A consumer's HARD-FK column to a CAT entity is `BIGINT`. An identifier is never reused or re-assigned.
- **Deactivation (RULE-CAT-048, ARCH-5).** A SHARED record is never deleted. Deactivation sets `isActiveFl` to false. The record stays readable by its identifier, leaves new selections, and raises a catalogue-change event with change kind DEACTIVATED. CAT notifies consumers and never cascades into them.
- **Tenancy.** The given core resolves the tenant. Every operation answers within the caller's tenant, and a record of another tenant reads as *not found*.
- **Names.** `nameAr` is always present. Where `nameEn` is empty, the English display returns `nameAr` (REQ-CAT-029).
- **Lookup codes.** Lookup-backed values are returned as codes of the CAT-owned `mdl` keys (SRS A6). Consumers resolve labels through the core lookup module. A code that is deactivated later stays on existing records (REQ-CAT-076).
- **Authorization of in-process calls (ADR-CAT-061).** The operation items are in-process calls. CAT does not check a CAT permission on them: the **calling module authorizes its own caller** against its own permissions before it calls CAT (for example SFR for a storefront shopper, SLS for a cashier). The one exception is the catalogue search (CON-CAT-016), which answers an empty result to a user without VIEW on CAT_ITEMS. Tenancy still applies to every call. Checklist C13 states no exception for an in-process read, so this rule stands on condition: CAT has raised `CORE-GAP-in-process-authorization.md`, and ADR-CAT-066 follows the core's answer (for example a core-provided caller authority for in-process reads) without changing these signatures.
- **Obligation of the capability-flag writer (POL-CAT-005).** A write that turns `variants` or `bundles` off must first call CON-CAT-017 and must refuse the write when `allowed` is false, showing the returned message and dependent Items. CAT answers the check and never writes a flag. The writer (owner OPEN, P-02) does not yet honour this, so it is recorded as an OPEN platform finding in `business-policies-cat.md` (PLATFORM FINDINGS, row 2).

---

## 1 — Entity items

### CON-CAT-001 — Item (صنف / Item)

Entity    : ENT-CAT-001 — key columns: code (business key, series CAT_ITEM, read-only), itemPk (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Traces    : ENT-CAT-001, REQ-CAT-001, REQ-CAT-032, REQ-CAT-034

Consumers store `itemPk`. `code` is unique per tenant and never changes (RULE-CAT-004). The values of `itemKindCode` (STANDARD / BUNDLE) and `baseUomCode` are fixed after first save (RULE-CAT-022, RULE-CAT-007).

### CON-CAT-002 — Variant (متغيّر الصنف / Variant)

Entity    : ENT-CAT-002 — key columns: code (business key, series CAT_VARIANT, read-only), variantPk (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Traces    : ENT-CAT-002, REQ-CAT-025, REQ-CAT-140, REQ-CAT-141, REQ-CAT-033, REQ-CAT-017

Stock, price, barcode and the e-invoice override attach to the Variant (D-01). Every Item has at least one Variant, and an active Item always has exactly one active default Variant (`isDefaultFl` true), the Variant stock and price consumers fall back to. When the default Variant is deactivated, the flag moves to the Item's remaining active Variant with the lowest code; when Variants are generated for an Item with no active Variant, the first Variant created becomes the default (REQ-CAT-025, ADR-CAT-072). A consumer therefore reads the default from the Variant reads and never assumes it is the Variant created with the Item. Turning `variants` on never changes an existing `variantPk`, code or barcode (REQ-CAT-017).

### CON-CAT-003 — Category (فئة / Category)

Entity    : ENT-CAT-006 — key columns: code (business key, unique per tenant, fixed after creation), categoryPk (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Traces    : ENT-CAT-006, REQ-CAT-064, REQ-CAT-067

The Category tree is at most 5 levels deep and has no cycles (RULE-CAT-023).

### CON-CAT-004 — Item unit (وحدة الصنف / Item unit)

Entity    : ENT-CAT-008 — key columns: itemFk + uomCode (business key among the Item's active units), itemUnitPk (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Traces    : ENT-CAT-008, REQ-CAT-077, REQ-CAT-081

`conversionFactor` is the number of base units in one unit of this kind (decimal, 6 places). It is fixed after saving (RULE-CAT-028), so a quantity that a consumer recorded keeps its meaning.

### CON-CAT-005 — Barcode (باركود / Barcode)

Entity    : ENT-CAT-009 — key columns: barcodeValue (business key, unique among the tenant's active barcodes, SCALE compared only with SCALE), barcodePk (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Traces    : ENT-CAT-009, REQ-CAT-083, REQ-CAT-087

Every barcode identifies one Variant in one unit (`uomCode`).

### CON-CAT-006 — Bundle component (مكوّن الحزمة / Bundle component)

Entity    : ENT-CAT-007 — key columns: bundleItemFk + componentVariantFk (business key among the Bundle's active lines), bundleComponentPk (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Traces    : ENT-CAT-007, REQ-CAT-056, REQ-CAT-058

A Bundle keeps no stock of its own; consumers count stock on the component Variants (DP §8 P-06). A line's `quantity` (decimal, 3 places) is always the number of **base units of the component Variant's Item** in one Bundle — never a quantity in one of that Item's units — and every read returns that unit as `componentBaseUomCode` (ADR-CAT-080). The base unit is fixed after the Item's first save (RULE-CAT-007), so a consumer that splits a sold Bundle into component movements multiplies the Bundle quantity by `quantity` and records the result in the component's base unit. Component Variants always belong to STANDARD Items, and Bundles are never nested (RULE-CAT-019).

---

## 2 — Operation items

### CON-CAT-007 — Read an Item (قراءة صنف / Read an Item)
Signature : getItem(itemPk: BIGINT) → Item {itemPk, code, nameAr, nameEn, itemKindCode, baseUomCode, categoryFk, brandCode, taxClassCode, einvoiceCodeTypeCode, einvoiceCode, publishStateCode, isActiveFl; active Variants with their attribute values; Item units; barcodes; Bundle component lines (componentVariantFk, Variant code, quantity, componentBaseUomCode — the base unit of the component Variant's Item, in which the quantity is expressed); active media} · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-CAT-001
Traces    : ENT-CAT-001, REQ-CAT-001, REQ-CAT-002, REQ-CAT-010, REQ-CAT-058

An inactive Item is still returned, with `isActiveFl` false. Every read that starts after a commit returns that commit's state (REQ-CAT-010).

### CON-CAT-008 — Read a Variant (قراءة متغيّر / Read a Variant)
Signature : getVariant(variantPk: BIGINT) → Variant {variantPk, itemFk, code, nameAr, nameEn (derived, D-15), isDefaultFl, isActiveFl, Item baseUomCode, Item taxClassCode, effective einvoiceCodeTypeCode and einvoiceCode (Variant override, else Item), einvoiceUnitCode of the base unit (empty when the unit has no active Unit e-invoice code)} · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-CAT-002
Traces    : ENT-CAT-002, REQ-CAT-050, REQ-CAT-119, REQ-CAT-122, REQ-CAT-029

An inactive Variant is still returned, with `isActiveFl` false (REQ-CAT-050).

### CON-CAT-009 — Read an Item unit (قراءة وحدة صنف / Read an Item unit)
Signature : getItemUnit(itemUnitPk: BIGINT) → Item unit {itemUnitPk, itemFk, uomCode, conversionFactor, isPurchaseFl, isSalesFl, isActiveFl, einvoiceUnitCode (empty when none is active)} · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-CAT-008
Traces    : ENT-CAT-008, REQ-CAT-079, REQ-CAT-122

### CON-CAT-010 — Look up a barcode or scale label (البحث بالباركود أو ملصق الميزان / Barcode and scale label lookup)
Signature : lookupBarcode(value: text) → {variantPk, itemPk, uomCode, embeddedValue (weight or price, with the layout's decimals; empty for a plain barcode), embeddedValueTypeCode (WEIGHT / PRICE; empty for a plain barcode)} · errors: not found — no active barcode or scale layout matches (RULE-CAT-047); scale label not readable — wrong check digit or unknown scale code (RULE-CAT-035)
Entity    : ENT-CAT-009
Traces    : ENT-CAT-009, REQ-CAT-086, REQ-CAT-090, REQ-CAT-097, REQ-CAT-100

Only active barcodes of active Variants of active Items, and active scale label layouts, match (REQ-CAT-085, REQ-CAT-094, REQ-CAT-086 — ADR-CAT-059).

### CON-CAT-011 — Read a Category (قراءة فئة / Read a Category)
Signature : getCategory(categoryPk: BIGINT) → Category {categoryPk, code, nameAr, nameEn, parentCategoryFk, sortOrder, isActiveFl} · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-CAT-006
Traces    : ENT-CAT-006, REQ-CAT-066, REQ-CAT-067

### CON-CAT-012 — Category tree (شجرة الفئات / Category tree)
Signature : getCategoryTree(activeOnly: flag) → tree of Category, each node under its parent and ordered by sortOrder · errors: none
Entity    : ENT-CAT-006
Traces    : ENT-CAT-006, REQ-CAT-067

### CON-CAT-013 — Items of a Category (أصناف الفئة / Items of a Category)
Signature : listCategoryItems(categoryPk: BIGINT, page, size) → page of active Items of the Category and of its active sub-categories · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-CAT-001
Traces    : ENT-CAT-001, ENT-CAT-006, REQ-CAT-068

### CON-CAT-014 — Storefront catalogue read (كتالوج المتجر الإلكتروني / Storefront catalogue)
Signature : listStorefrontItems(page, size) → page of Items with isActiveFl true and publishStateCode PUBLISHED · errors: none
Entity    : ENT-CAT-001
Traces    : ENT-CAT-001, REQ-CAT-115, REQ-CAT-113, REQ-CAT-114

### CON-CAT-015 — Images of a Variant (صور المتغيّر / Images of a Variant)
Signature : getVariantImages(variantPk: BIGINT) → list of image {fileDocumentRef, nameAr, nameEn, isPrimaryFl, sortOrder} — the active images bound to the Variant, or else the Item's active images bound to no Variant, primary first · errors: not found
Entity    : ENT-CAT-002
Traces    : ENT-CAT-002, REQ-CAT-109

`fileDocumentRef` points to the given `file` public image storage (`erp-core:modules.file`).

### CON-CAT-016 — Catalogue search for the global search (بحث الكتالوج / Catalogue search)
Signature : searchCatalogue(term: text) → at most 20 active Items {itemPk, code, nameAr, nameEn} whose nameAr, nameEn, code or active barcode contains the term, exact code or barcode matches first · errors: term too short — fewer than 2 characters (RULE-CAT-046); forbidden — the caller lacks VIEW on CAT_ITEMS, answered as an empty result
Entity    : ENT-CAT-001
Traces    : ENT-CAT-001, REQ-CAT-125, REQ-CAT-126, REQ-CAT-127

The global search box sits outside CAT (ADR-CAT-043).

### CON-CAT-017 — Capability turn-off check (فحص إيقاف القدرة / Capability turn-off check)
Signature : checkCapabilityTurnOff(flag: `variants` | `bundles`) → {allowed: flag, dependentCount: number, dependentItems: at most 20 {itemPk, code, nameAr}, messageAr and messageEn (the RULE-CAT-010 or RULE-CAT-011 message with {count} filled when not allowed; empty when allowed)} · errors: invalid input — flag other than `variants` or `bundles`
Entity    : ENT-CAT-001
Traces    : ENT-CAT-001, REQ-CAT-021, REQ-CAT-022, REQ-CAT-023

This check is offered to the capability-flag owner (owner OPEN, P-02 — ADR-CAT-041). CAT does not own or change the flags.

---

## 3 — Event item

### CON-CAT-018 — Catalogue-change event (إشعار تغيير الكتالوج / Catalogue-change event)
Signature : catalogueChanged(entity: ENT id of the changed record — ENT-CAT-001, 002, 005, 006, 007, 008, 009 or 010; recordPk: BIGINT; itemPk: BIGINT (empty when entity is ENT-CAT-006); changeKind: CAT_CHANGE_KIND — CREATED / UPDATED / DEACTIVATED / REACTIVATED) → one event per committed change, delivered after commit · errors: none — a rolled-back change publishes no event (RULE-CAT-049)
Entity    : ENT-CAT-001
Traces    : ENT-CAT-001, ENT-CAT-002, ENT-CAT-006, REQ-CAT-008, REQ-CAT-009

The event is recorded in the same transaction as the change through the given `events` outbox, and it is delivered only after commit. Consumers read the current state through CON-CAT-007 … CON-CAT-015; the event carries no field values.

A Category (ENT-CAT-006) is a SHARED record read by SFR and INS, so its creation, change (including a move under another parent), deactivation and reactivation each publish one event with `entity` ENT-CAT-006, `recordPk` = categoryPk and an empty `itemPk`; consumers re-read it through CON-CAT-011 / CON-CAT-012 (ADR-CAT-077). Adding ENT-CAT-006 to the entity list is ADDITIVE: the payload keeps its fields and types, and no consumer edge cites the item yet (§6).

---

## 4 — Given usage (repeated verbatim from SRS A9)

```yaml name=given-usage
rows:
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.mdl, via: com.erp.mdl.crossmodule.MdlLookupApi, purpose: "load the values of the CAT_* lookup keys (brand, unit of measure, tax class, e-invoice codes, barcode type, scale value type, item kind, publish state) at runtime"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.mdl, via: com.erp.mdl.crossmodule.MdlLookupApi, purpose: "serve CAT's own lookup keys to the screens (API-CAT-052) through the common owned-lookups capability, com.erp.common.lookup.OwnedLookups.read(key, the 10 CAT keys, CAT-404-LOOKUP-KEY-UNKNOWN, MdlLookupApi::readActiveValuesByKey) — never a pass-through for other keys (ADR-CAT-065); erp-core's manifest lists owned-lookups under common but publishes no interface for it, so the row names the mdl interface the call goes through"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.cu, via: com.erp.cu.crossmodule.SettingsApi, purpose: "read the `variants` and `bundles` capability flags behind the FeatureFlags API (ADR-CAT-041)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sequence, via: com.erp.sequence.crossmodule.NumberSeriesApi, purpose: "issue Item codes (CAT_ITEM), Variant codes (CAT_VARIANT) and internal barcode digits (CAT_INTERNAL_BARCODE)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.file, via: docs/api-docs/file/index.md, purpose: "the client uploads Item images to the FILE category CAT_ITEM_IMAGE (allowPublic = true, PNG / JPEG / WEBP, at most 5 MB — the FILE endpoint refuses any other file, ADR-CAT-076) and import files to the private storage through the given FILE upload endpoints, and passes the returned FILE_DOCUMENT.ID to CAT; CAT has no multipart endpoint (ADR-CAT-056)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.file, via: com.erp.file.crossmodule.FileDocumentLookupApi, purpose: "the published methods only: isAvailable (a referenced file exists and is available in the caller's tenant — API-CAT-018, API-CAT-049), publicUrl and publicUrls (an image has a public URL, and image URLs for reads); the type, size and bytes of an import file are not published and wait on CORE-GAP-file-document-metadata-read (ADR-CAT-076)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.file, via: com.erp.file.crossmodule.FilePrivateStoreApi, purpose: "the private store that holds the uploaded xlsx import file (SCR-REQ-CAT-004, ADR-CAT-056), named in CORE-GAP-file-document-metadata-read as the candidate in-process read of that file's type, size and bytes for the import (RULE-CAT-044, API-CAT-049); CAT stores no file through it (the client uploads through the FILE endpoints) and wires the read when the core publishes it (ADR-CAT-076)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.events, via: com.erp.events.OutboxPublisher, purpose: "record catalogue-change events in the change transaction and deliver them after commit (RULE-CAT-049)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.report, via: com.erp.report.crossmodule.PrintApi, purpose: "produce the printable barcode label document (REQ-CAT-102) and the xlsx item import sheet (REQ-CAT-036)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.report, via: com.erp.report.ReportProvider, purpose: "register the catalogue reports CAT_BARCODE_LABEL and CAT_ITEM_IMPORT_SHEET (ADR-CAT-057)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sec, via: com.erp.sec.permission.PermissionContributor, purpose: "register the CAT page codes so the security module derives their VIEW/CREATE/UPDATE/DELETE permissions"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.tenant, via: com.erp.tenant.TenantProvisioningContributor, purpose: "copy the 8 seeded Unit e-invoice codes into each newly provisioned tenant and register the FILE category CAT_ITEM_IMAGE there (ADR-CAT-076); the given copies the CAT lookup values and number series itself (ADR-CAT-058, REQ-CAT-134)"}
```

The contract does not promise any entity that a given provides. Tenant, User, Role, LookupType/LookupValue, FileDocument and NumberSeries stay with `erp-core` [C:C-GIVEN-1]. A new tenant receives the CAT seeds through the given provisioning (REQ-CAT-134, ADR-CAT-058), so every operation item works in every tenant from its first request.

---

## 5 — Stability

- The items above are ADDITIVE in CS-CAT-001. Later versions may add items freely.
- Changing or removing any CON-CAT item, a key column, or the BIGINT identifier type is legal only in a BREAKING change set with an ADR. `gov.py graph` then raises a resolution event on every consumer edge that cites the item.

## 6 — Self-check

- [x] Every SHARED ENT of the SRS has an entity item (ENT-CAT-001, 002, 006, 007, 008, 009). No consumer's A8 block exists yet that names any other CAT entity, and no PRIVATE entity is promised.
- [x] Every operation item has a `Signature` line and names no path or verb.
- [x] Every item traces to at least one ENT and one REQ (C13.5).
- [x] No question was raised. The CON sequence starts at 001 because the current state holds no earlier contract, and it runs 001 … 018 without gaps (next free: CON-CAT-019). The contract itself raised no ADR; ADR-CAT-072 (gate-analysis round 6) amends the wording of CON-CAT-002 without changing its key columns or identifier type, and ADR-CAT-077 (gate-analysis round 9) adds ENT-CAT-006 to the entity list of CON-CAT-018 without changing its fields. ADR-CAT-078 (gate-analysis round 10) adds an operation for the barcode label screen and changes no contract item. ADR-CAT-079 (gate-analysis round 11) narrows the barcode printed on a label and changes no contract item; ADR-CAT-080 (same round) states the unit of a Bundle component quantity in CON-CAT-006 and adds `componentBaseUomCode` to the component lines of CON-CAT-007, without changing a key column or identifier type (ADDITIVE). Per the current registries the next free ADR is ADR-CAT-081.
- [x] §4 carries the SRS `given-usage` rows verbatim, in the same order.
- [x] Every item title carries ar and en.

<<<contract-pty.md>>>
# CONTRACT — Party / الأطراف (PTY) — outbound promise
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Module : PTY   Version : v1   Profile : commerce   Stage : P1.5
Inputs : srs (analysis/modules/PTY/P1/srs-pty.md, P1 commit f0653ef), registry-srs
Counts : CON 24 (ENTITY 5 · LOOKUP 3 · OPERATION 14 · EVENT 1 · EXTENSION POINT 1) · new ADR 0
Status : DRAFT — P1.5 pass 1 · gate-analysis revise (ADR-PTY-037, 038, 039, 041, 042 — additive items and error meanings; no consumer is built yet) · gate-analysis revise 2 (ADR-PTY-045, 046, 048 — one error meaning on CON-PTY-016, one output field on CON-PTY-020, the check order of CON-PTY-015; all additive)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## 0 — Scope of this promise

This contract states what other modules may depend on before Party is built. It promises;
it never implements. The backend stage (P3.1) chooses paths and verbs and states
`Honours: CON-PTY-…` on every block that implements an item.

| Rule | Applied here |
|---|---|
| Identifiers | Every entity identifier is a BIGINT identity key the database assigns (`stack.db.pk_generation = identity`). A consumer's HARD-FK column to a PTY entity is `BIGINT`, copied verbatim. No other identifier type exists in v1. |
| Business keys | Party `code` (from given `sequence`, read-only after first save — RULE-PTY-007) is the business key Customer and Supplier present; Credit override request `docNo` (from given `sequence`, read-only — RULE-PTY-031). |
| Capability flag | Credit items read `b2bCredit` through the FeatureFlags API only; while it is off, credit fields are omitted and credit operations answer as stated per item (REQ-PTY-070 … REQ-PTY-074). |
| Currency | Every amount is in the tenant's base currency (SRS A2 scope exception: no multi-currency party balances). Each credit amount carries the code of that currency (`currencyCode`, ISO 4217 — ADR-PTY-037); a consumer converts its own amounts into it before a credit check, a request or a use. |
| Authority | Every operation item is served by a PTY service method that checks its own PTY permission; an in-process caller's security context must hold it (ADR-PTY-039). |
| Errors | Named by meaning; each carries the bilingual message of the cited RULE. Access refusals come from the platform authorisation layer ("forbidden"). |
| Not promised | Party create/update by consumers other than registration in place, Customer/Supplier search and maintenance screens, address maintenance, Customer credit maintenance, credit history, credit-overrun setting, approve / reject / cancel of Credit override requests, CUSTOMER-realm invitation handling — these are internal to PTY (SCR-REQ-PTY-001 … SCR-REQ-PTY-005). |

### Consumer index (from SRS A3 Ownership and A8.1)

| Consumer | Items it may depend on |
|---|---|
| SLS — Sales & POS | CON-PTY-001, 002, 004, 005, 006, 007, 008, 009, 011, 012, 013, 015, 016, 017, 018, 020, 021, 022, 023 |
| PUR — Purchasing | CON-PTY-001, 003, 004, 007, 010, 011, 012, 021, 024 |
| STL — Settlements | CON-PTY-001, 002, 003, 009, 010, 021, 022 |
| TAX — Tax & E-invoicing | CON-PTY-001, 002, 003, 009, 010 |
| SFR — Storefront | CON-PTY-001, 002, 004, 005, 007, 008, 009, 011, 012, 014, 015, 016, 017, 018, 021 |
| PRC — Pricing | CON-PTY-002, 006, 009 |
| Capability-flag owner (OPEN — registry OI-05) | CON-PTY-019 |

Party sits at DAG #4 with `consumes: []` (SRS A8); every consumer above sits below it, so
every edge points into PTY.

---

# PART 1 — ENTITY ITEMS

### CON-PTY-001 — الطرف / Party (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-PTY-001 — key columns: code (business key, text ≤ 20, from given `sequence`, read-only), partyPk (identifier) · identifier type: BIGINT
Promised  : one Party per counterparty, holding nameAr, nameEn, mobile, email and taxRegistrationNo once, shared by its Customer and its Supplier (REQ-PTY-024). Consumers reach the Party through the Customer / Supplier reads (CON-PTY-009, CON-PTY-010) and the address read (CON-PTY-011); a consumer storing a Party reference stores partyPk.
Traces    : ENT-PTY-001, REQ-PTY-001, REQ-PTY-006, REQ-PTY-024

### CON-PTY-002 — العميل / Customer (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-PTY-002 — key columns: code (business key, presented from ENT-PTY-001.code), customerPk (identifier — the HARD-FK target for SLS, STL, SFR) · identifier type: BIGINT
Promised  : one Customer record per Party, with the same identifier for back-office, point of sale and storefront (REQ-PTY-001); partyFk never changes; customerGroupCode always present (REQ-PTY-039); isWalkInFl marks the tenant's single walk-in Customer (RULE-PTY-014); currencyCode names the currency of the Customer's credit amounts (ADR-PTY-037); a deactivated Customer stays readable by every existing reference (REQ-PTY-014).
Traces    : ENT-PTY-002, REQ-PTY-001, REQ-PTY-002, REQ-PTY-014, REQ-PTY-039

### CON-PTY-003 — المورّد / Supplier (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-PTY-003 — key columns: code (business key, presented from ENT-PTY-001.code), supplierPk (identifier — the HARD-FK target for PUR, STL) · identifier type: BIGINT
Promised  : one Supplier record per Party; partyFk never changes; paymentTermDays is independent of `b2bCredit` (ADR-PTY-012); a deactivated Supplier stays readable by every existing reference (REQ-PTY-020).
Traces    : ENT-PTY-003, REQ-PTY-019, REQ-PTY-020

### CON-PTY-004 — العنوان / Address (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-PTY-004 — key columns: partyFk + addressTypeCode + code (business key: ordinal within the Party, not a business number), addressPk (identifier — the HARD-FK target for SLS, PUR, SFR documents) · identifier type: BIGINT
Promised  : addresses belong to a Party (shared by its Customer and Supplier); one active default per Party per address type while the Party has an active Address of that type (RULE-PTY-017); a deactivated Address stays readable by its identifier (REQ-PTY-033).
Traces    : ENT-PTY-004, REQ-PTY-030, REQ-PTY-032, REQ-PTY-033, REQ-PTY-034

### CON-PTY-005 — طلب تجاوز حد الائتمان / Credit override request (ar / en)

Entity    : ENT-PTY-005 — key columns: docNo (business key, text ≤ 20, from given `sequence`, read-only), creditOverrideRequestPk (identifier — the reference SLS and SFR store against a credit sale) · identifier type: BIGINT
Promised  : one open request (PENDING or APPROVED) per Customer and credit sale above the limit (RULE-PTY-034), status lifecycle PENDING → APPROVED → USED, PENDING → REJECTED, PENDING → CANCELLED, and PENDING or APPROVED → CANCELLED when the Customer is deactivated (SRS A7, REQ-PTY-111); the sale is held by text reference (sourceDocumentRef, usedDocumentRef — ADR-PTY-016), so the consumer passes its own document reference and PTY stores no key into the consumer; a use must carry the reference the request was raised for (ADR-PTY-042); amounts carry currencyCode. Exists only while `b2bCredit` is on (RULE-PTY-023).
Traces    : ENT-PTY-005, REQ-PTY-091, REQ-PTY-092, REQ-PTY-097, REQ-PTY-103, REQ-PTY-111, REQ-PTY-113

---

# PART 2 — LOOKUP ITEMS

Values are loaded at runtime from given `mdl` (profile LOOKUPS); consumers read them through
given `mdl`, never as constants, except the closed values they branch on (CON-PTY-008).

### CON-PTY-006 — مجموعة العملاء / Customer group lookup (ar / en)

Lookup    : CUSTOMER_GROUP (open; seeded RETAIL, WHOLESALE) — key column: value code, carried as ENT-PTY-002.customerGroupCode
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002 — key columns: customerGroupCode
Promised  : every Customer holds exactly one active value; RETAIL is the default (REQ-PTY-007). PRC keys price lists on this code.
Traces    : ENT-PTY-002, REQ-PTY-007, REQ-PTY-039, REQ-PTY-040

### CON-PTY-007 — نوع العنوان / Address type lookup (ar / en)

Lookup    : ADDRESS_TYPE (open; seeded BILLING, DELIVERY) — key column: value code, carried as ENT-PTY-004.addressTypeCode
Entity    : ENT-PTY-004 — key columns: addressTypeCode
Promised  : every Address holds one active value; a guest's address is created as DELIVERY (REQ-PTY-050).
Traces    : ENT-PTY-004, REQ-PTY-038, REQ-PTY-050

### CON-PTY-008 — رموز ضبط الائتمان / Credit control codes (ar / en)

Lookup    : CREDIT_CHECK_RESULT (closed: WITHIN_LIMIT, OVER_LIMIT, NO_CREDIT, EXPOSURE_UNAVAILABLE, NOT_APPLICABLE) · CREDIT_OVERRUN_ACTION (closed: WARN, BLOCK_WITH_OVERRIDE) · CREDIT_OVERRIDE_STATUS (closed: PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED, CANCELLED, USED)
Entity    : ENT-PTY-005 — key columns: creditCheckResultCode, statusCode
Promised  : closed value sets — a consumer may branch on these codes; a new value is a BREAKING change (§ Stability).
Traces    : ENT-PTY-005, REQ-PTY-074, REQ-PTY-081, REQ-PTY-082, REQ-PTY-083, REQ-PTY-084, REQ-PTY-086, REQ-PTY-094

---

# PART 3 — OPERATION ITEMS

### CON-PTY-009 — قراءة العميل / Read Customer (ar / en)

Signature : readCustomer(customerId: BIGINT, forNewDocument: flag) → Customer { customerPk, partyFk, code, nameAr, nameEn, mobile, email, taxRegistrationNo, customerGroupCode, isWalkInFl, isActiveFl, creditLimit, paymentTermDays, currencyCode } · errors: not found; inactive for a new document (RULE-PTY-005, when forNewDocument is set); forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002
Notes     : creditLimit, paymentTermDays and currencyCode are present only while `b2bCredit` is on and are omitted otherwise (REQ-PTY-071). An inactive Customer is returned when forNewDocument is not set (existing references stay readable, REQ-PTY-014). partyFk is part of the Customer entity returned (SRS A8.1 output "Customer") and is the input of CON-PTY-011. The three credit values are returned to every caller holding Customer VIEW; the credit standing with exposure is not part of this item (ADR-PTY-046).
Traces    : REQ-PTY-002, REQ-PTY-003, REQ-PTY-014, REQ-PTY-071

### CON-PTY-010 — قراءة المورّد / Read Supplier (ar / en)

Signature : readSupplier(supplierId: BIGINT, forNewDocument: flag) → Supplier { supplierPk, partyFk, code, nameAr, nameEn, mobile, email, taxRegistrationNo, paymentTermDays, isActiveFl } · errors: not found; inactive for a new document (RULE-PTY-005, when forNewDocument is set); forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-003
Traces    : REQ-PTY-019, REQ-PTY-003, REQ-PTY-020

### CON-PTY-011 — عناوين الطرف / Read Party addresses (ar / en)

Signature : listPartyAddresses(partyId: BIGINT) → list of Address { addressPk, partyFk, addressTypeCode, code, nameAr, nameEn, line1, line2, city, region, postalCode, isDefaultFl } — active Addresses only · errors: Party not found; forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-004
Traces    : REQ-PTY-032, REQ-PTY-034, REQ-PTY-035

### CON-PTY-012 — قراءة العنوان / Read Address (ar / en)

Signature : readAddress(addressId: BIGINT) → Address { addressPk, partyFk, addressTypeCode, code, nameAr, nameEn, line1, line2, city, region, postalCode, isDefaultFl, isActiveFl } — inactive Addresses included · errors: not found; forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-004
Notes     : lets a document that stored addressPk show it after deactivation (AC-PTY-039).
Traces    : REQ-PTY-033

### CON-PTY-013 — العميل النقدي / Default Customer for a point-of-sale sale (ar / en)

Signature : defaultPosCustomer() → Customer (as CON-PTY-009, isWalkInFl true) · errors: forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002
Notes     : always answers — the walk-in Customer is created on first demand when the tenant has none, and repeated calls return the same identifier (REQ-PTY-046, RULE-PTY-014). The walk-in Customer is never inactive (RULE-PTY-010), carries no credit (RULE-PTY-011) and cannot receive a Credit override request (RULE-PTY-033).
Traces    : REQ-PTY-044, REQ-PTY-045, REQ-PTY-046, REQ-PTY-047

### CON-PTY-014 — بيانات طلب الزائر / Guest order customer resolution (ar / en)

Signature : resolveGuestCustomer(nameAr, nameEn, email, mobile, deliveryAddress { line1, line2, city, region, postalCode }) → customerId: BIGINT · errors: contact required (RULE-PTY-019); name required (RULE-PTY-001); email or mobile format (RULE-PTY-003, RULE-PTY-004); order cannot be completed with these details (RULE-PTY-021 — does not reveal that a matching Customer exists)
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002, ENT-PTY-001, ENT-PTY-004
Notes     : matches an active Party by email (letter case ignored), then by a normalised mobile held by exactly one active Party; otherwise creates Party + Customer (RETAIL) + default DELIVERY Address. Adds a Customer role to a matched Party holding none. Guest details never change a matched Party. The walk-in Party and inactive Parties are never matched. The CUSTOMER-realm invitation is PTY's own follow-up through given `sec`, not part of the answer.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-050, REQ-PTY-053, REQ-PTY-055, REQ-PTY-056, REQ-PTY-057, REQ-PTY-058, REQ-PTY-061, REQ-PTY-062, REQ-PTY-063

### CON-PTY-015 — فحص الائتمان / Credit check (ar / en)

Signature : checkCredit(customerId: BIGINT, saleAmount: decimal) → CreditCheck { resultCode (CREDIT_CHECK_RESULT), creditLimit, openExposure, projectedExposure, currencyCode, overrunActionCode (CREDIT_OVERRUN_ACTION), approvalRequired: flag } · errors: Customer not found; Customer inactive (RULE-PTY-005, REQ-PTY-110); forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002
Notes     : The Customer is checked first, whatever the flag: not found, then inactive (RULE-PTY-005). For an active Customer, `b2bCredit` off → NOT_APPLICABLE. Empty creditLimit → NO_CREDIT. projectedExposure = openExposure + saleAmount; ≤ creditLimit → WITHIN_LIMIT, > creditLimit → OVER_LIMIT. A contributor not answering within 3 s → EXPOSURE_UNAVAILABLE with openExposure empty. OVER_LIMIT, NO_CREDIT and EXPOSURE_UNAVAILABLE carry the tenant's overrun action (default BLOCK_WITH_OVERRIDE) and approvalRequired = yes under BLOCK_WITH_OVERRIDE, no under WARN.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-068, REQ-PTY-074, REQ-PTY-079, REQ-PTY-080, REQ-PTY-081, REQ-PTY-082, REQ-PTY-083, REQ-PTY-084, REQ-PTY-087, REQ-PTY-088, REQ-PTY-089, REQ-PTY-110

### CON-PTY-016 — طلب موافقة على تجاوز الائتمان / Create Credit override request (ar / en)

Signature : createCreditOverrideRequest(customerId: BIGINT, saleAmount: decimal, sourceDocumentRef: text ≤ 60) → CreditOverrideRequest (as CON-PTY-017, statusCode PENDING) · errors: credit not enabled (RULE-PTY-023); sale within the limit — no approval needed (RULE-PTY-025); sale amount not greater than zero (RULE-PTY-032); walk-in Customer cannot buy on credit (RULE-PTY-033); Customer inactive (RULE-PTY-005); this sale already has an open request — names its docNo (RULE-PTY-034); the overrun action is Warn only, so no approval is required (RULE-PTY-035); Customer not found; forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-005
Notes     : raised by the consumer when CON-PTY-015 answered approvalRequired = yes; accepted only while the tenant's credit-overrun action is BLOCK_WITH_OVERRIDE (ADR-PTY-045). PTY fills docNo, docDate, creditLimitAtRequest, openExposure, creditCheckResultCode and the requester, and notifies the approvers.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-072, REQ-PTY-089, REQ-PTY-091, REQ-PTY-092, REQ-PTY-093, REQ-PTY-104, REQ-PTY-105, REQ-PTY-110, REQ-PTY-113, REQ-PTY-114

### CON-PTY-017 — قراءة طلب تجاوز الائتمان / Read Credit override request (ar / en)

Signature : readCreditOverrideRequest(creditOverrideRequestId: BIGINT) → CreditOverrideRequest { creditOverrideRequestPk, docNo, docDate, statusCode (CREDIT_OVERRIDE_STATUS), customerFk, saleAmount, currencyCode, sourceDocumentRef, decidedBy, decidedAt, decisionReason } · errors: not found; forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-005
Traces    : REQ-PTY-103

### CON-PTY-018 — استخدام الموافقة / Use Credit override request (ar / en)

Signature : useCreditOverrideRequest(creditOverrideRequestId: BIGINT, customerId: BIGINT, saleAmount: decimal, usedDocumentRef: text ≤ 60) → CreditOverrideRequest (statusCode USED) · errors: approval does not cover this sale — not APPROVED, another Customer, another sale reference than sourceDocumentRef, or a larger amount (RULE-PTY-028); Customer inactive (RULE-PTY-005); credit not enabled (RULE-PTY-023); not found; forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-005
Notes     : one approval covers one sale — the sale it was requested for (usedDocumentRef equals sourceDocumentRef, ADR-PTY-042); a second use is refused (AC-PTY-113); an approval of a Customer deactivated meanwhile is CANCELLED and refused (AC-PTY-129).
Traces    : REQ-PTY-097, REQ-PTY-099, REQ-PTY-110, REQ-PTY-111, REQ-PTY-112

### CON-PTY-019 — اعتماد إيقاف الائتمان / `b2bCredit` switch-off dependency (ar / en)

Signature : b2bCreditSwitchOffDependency() → { dependentCustomerCount: number } · errors: forbidden
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002
Notes     : consumer is the capability-flag owner (registry OI-05, still OPEN). A count above 0 means the switch-off must be refused with the message of RULE-PTY-024 and the next action "Show customers with credit" / «عرض العملاء ذوي الائتمان»; 0 means PTY does not refuse.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-075, REQ-PTY-076, REQ-PTY-077

### CON-PTY-020 — البحث الشامل عن العملاء / Customer global search (ar / en)

Signature : globalSearchCustomers(term: text) → list of at most 10 Customer { customerPk, code, nameAr, nameEn, mobile, isWalkInFl } · errors: none — a term under 2 characters or a user without VIEW on PTY_CUSTOMER yields an empty list
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002
Notes     : active Customers only; matches code, nameAr, nameEn, mobile, email or taxRegistrationNo without letter case and with Arabic letter forms unified; exact code matches first, then name ascending. Suppliers are not returned (ADR-PTY-013). The walk-in Customer is returned and marked by isWalkInFl, so a picker can hide it. Spaces and dashes are ignored in the mobile comparison.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-041, REQ-PTY-042, REQ-PTY-043

### CON-PTY-021 — حدث إيقاف صفة الطرف / Party role deactivated event (ar / en)

Signature : event `pty.PartyRoleDeactivated` → { role: CUSTOMER | SUPPLIER, identifier: BIGINT (customerPk or supplierPk), code } — published through the given outbox (`OutboxPublisher`) inside the deactivation transaction, delivered after commit, at least once · errors: —
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002, ENT-PTY-003
Notes     : prevent new use + notify; no cascade (RULE-PTY-006). Consumers keep their existing documents unchanged. A consumer subscribes with an `OutboxSubscriber` for type `pty.PartyRoleDeactivated` and must be idempotent on `OutboxMessage.id()`; a module enabled later receives the past events by replay (ADR-PTY-038).
Traces    : REQ-PTY-023

### CON-PTY-022 — نقطة امتداد مديونية العميل / Customer credit exposure extension point (ar / en)

Signature : contributor CustomerCreditExposureContributor.openExposure(customerId: BIGINT) → amount: decimal (in the Customer's currencyCode — the tenant base currency, ADR-PTY-037) · errors: no answer within 3 s → the credit check answers EXPOSURE_UNAVAILABLE
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002
Notes     : declared by PTY, implemented by downstream modules (STL receivables, SLS open orders); PTY sums every registered contributor's amount, 0 when none is registered (ADR-PTY-017). Registering a contributor creates no dependency from PTY on the contributor.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-080, REQ-PTY-084

### CON-PTY-023 — تسجيل عميل من مستند / Register Customer in place (ar / en)

Signature : registerCustomer(nameAr, nameEn, mobile, email, taxRegistrationNo, customerGroupCode) → { customerPk, partyFk, code } · errors: name required (RULE-PTY-001); email already held by another active Party — names its code (RULE-PTY-002); email format (RULE-PTY-003); mobile format (RULE-PTY-004); customer group not in the list (RULE-PTY-018); forbidden (CREATE on PTY_CUSTOMER)
Entity    : ENT-PTY-002, ENT-PTY-001
Notes     : one of nameAr / nameEn is the only mandatory input; customerGroupCode defaults to RETAIL; the code comes from given `sequence`. Used by a sale to register a Customer without leaving it.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-004, REQ-PTY-005, REQ-PTY-006, REQ-PTY-007, REQ-PTY-008, REQ-PTY-009, REQ-PTY-010, REQ-PTY-011

### CON-PTY-024 — تسجيل مورّد من مستند / Register Supplier in place (ar / en)

Signature : registerSupplier(nameAr, nameEn, mobile, email, taxRegistrationNo, paymentTermDays) → { supplierPk, partyFk, code } · errors: name required (RULE-PTY-001); email already held by another active Party — names its code (RULE-PTY-002); email format (RULE-PTY-003); mobile format (RULE-PTY-004); payment terms outside 0 – 365 (RULE-PTY-008); forbidden (CREATE on PTY_SUPPLIER)
Entity    : ENT-PTY-003, ENT-PTY-001
Notes     : one of nameAr / nameEn is the only mandatory input; the code comes from given `sequence`. Used by a purchase document to register a Supplier without leaving it.
Traces    : REQ-PTY-008, REQ-PTY-009, REQ-PTY-010, REQ-PTY-011, REQ-PTY-016, REQ-PTY-022

---

# PART 4 — GIVEN USAGE (repeated verbatim from SRS A9)

```yaml name=given-usage
rows:
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sec, via: com.erp.sec.crossmodule.SecCustomerInvitationApi, purpose: "Issue the CUSTOMER-realm invitation for a Customer created from or matched to a guest order, with PTY's template, from PTY's after-commit listener (REQ-PTY-051, REQ-PTY-059)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sec, via: com.erp.sec.crossmodule.SecUserDirectoryApi, purpose: "Resolve the CUSTOMER-realm user linked to a Customer and the users holding UPDATE on PTY_CREDIT_OVERRIDE; show user names in credit history and request decisions (REQ-PTY-093, REQ-PTY-108)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sec, via: com.erp.sec.permission.PermissionContributor, purpose: "PtyPermissions declares permission module PTY with the five PTY screens (SEC_PAGES) and their VIEW / CREATE / UPDATE / DELETE actions; every service method checks its constant (B4, ADR-PTY-039)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.cu, via: com.erp.cu.crossmodule.SettingsApi, purpose: "Read capability flag b2bCredit through the FeatureFlags API, read / save the tenant's credit-overrun setting and read the tenant base currency PTY_BASE_CURRENCY_CODE (REQ-PTY-070 … REQ-PTY-074, REQ-PTY-086, REQ-PTY-087; ADR-PTY-037)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.mdl, via: com.erp.mdl.crossmodule.MdlLookupApi, purpose: "Load CUSTOMER_GROUP, ADDRESS_TYPE, CREDIT_OVERRUN_ACTION, CREDIT_CHECK_RESULT and CREDIT_OVERRIDE_STATUS values at runtime to validate the stored codes, and serve these five PTY keys to PTY's screens through the common helper OwnedLookups.read over this interface (A6; ADR-PTY-048)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.sequence, via: com.erp.sequence.crossmodule.NumberSeriesApi, purpose: "Assign the Party code and the Credit override request docNo from the series PTY_PARTY and PTY_CREDIT_OVERRIDE seeded by the PTY migration (REQ-PTY-006, REQ-PTY-092)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.tenant, via: com.erp.tenant.TenantProvisioningContributor, purpose: "Create the tenant's walk-in Customer when a tenant is provisioned (REQ-PTY-044)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.tenant, via: com.erp.tenant.TenantRunner, purpose: "Re-bind the tenant of PTY's after-commit work through the given's published tenant runner on the given event executor (REQ-PTY-051, REQ-PTY-059, REQ-PTY-093; ADR-PTY-044)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.audit, via: com.erp.audit.crossmodule.AuditApi, purpose: "Record credit-limit and payment-term changes through the @Audited listener of Customer, record approve / reject decisions as business actions, and read the credit history back (REQ-PTY-106 … REQ-PTY-109)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.events, via: com.erp.events.OutboxPublisher, purpose: "Publish pty.PartyRoleDeactivated to consuming modules inside the deactivation transaction (REQ-PTY-023, ADR-PTY-038)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.events, via: com.erp.events.OutboxSubscriber, purpose: "Consume sec.CustomerInvitationRedeemed, idempotent on OutboxMessage.id(), to set secUserId (REQ-PTY-052, REQ-PTY-054)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.events, via: com.erp.events.DomainEventPublisher, purpose: "Publish PTY's own in-process events that only PTY's after-commit listeners consume — invitation issue and approver notification — never an event for another module (REQ-PTY-051, REQ-PTY-059, REQ-PTY-093)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.notif, via: com.erp.notif.crossmodule.NotificationDispatchApi, purpose: "Dispatch template PTY_CREDIT_OVERRIDE_REQUESTED, seeded by the PTY migration, to each approver of a new Credit override request (REQ-PTY-093, ADR-PTY-036)"}
  - {given: erp-core, module: modules.module, via: docs/api-docs/module/index.md, purpose: "Declare PTY to the given module catalogue with the module manifest META-INF/erp/modules/pty.yaml in the format the given's module api documents: code, package, permission module, api-paths, settings, events and migration (ADR-PTY-044)"}
```

No contract item re-creates a given entity: CUSTOMER-realm users stay in given `sec` (the
Customer holds only `secUserId`, which is not promised to consumers), the credit-overrun
setting stays in given `cu`, lookup values in given `mdl`, numbers in given `sequence`.

---

# PART 5 — STABILITY

| Change | Kind | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| New CON item, new optional input, new output field | ADDITIVE | allowed in any version |
| New value in an open lookup (CUSTOMER_GROUP, ADDRESS_TYPE) | ADDITIVE | data, not a contract change |
| New value in a closed set of CON-PTY-008 | BREAKING | new version + ADR |
| Removing or renaming an item, an input, an output field or an error meaning; changing an identifier type or a key column | BREAKING | new version + ADR; `gov.py graph` raises a resolution event on every consumer edge that cited the item |

---

# PART 6 — SELF-CHECK

- [x] Every SHARED entity of SRS A3 (ENT-PTY-001 … ENT-PTY-005) has an ENTITY item (CON-PTY-001 … CON-PTY-005); no other module's A8 block is in this stage's inputs, and PTY's own A8 is `consumes: []`.
- [x] Every operation of SRS A8.1 has an item (read Customer → 009, read Supplier → 010, read Party addresses → 011, default Customer → 013, guest order details → 014, credit check → 015, create / read / use Credit override request → 016 / 017 / 018, switch-off dependency → 019, global search → 020); the declared extension point → 022 and the domain event → 021; registration in place (REQ-PTY-008) → 023, 024; read Address by identifier (AC-PTY-039) → 012.
- [x] Every operation item has a `Signature` line and names no path or verb.
- [x] Every item traces to an ENT or REQ; no private entity is promised.
- [x] Identifier type stated once per entity item as BIGINT.
- [x] No question raised; CON sequence 001 … 024 continuous; no ADR needed (every item rests on the SRS).
- [x] Part 4 carries the SRS's `given-usage` rows verbatim, same order.
